  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献用原始文本与重写文本之间的语义相似度来测量变换对水印的侵蚀程度，但作者论证端点相似度是错误的统计量——它无法捕捉变换路径中由稳定子和乐携带的信息。

Method: 借用语言学循环（linguistic loops）的形式体系，证明保持语义的变换链的不变量可典范分解为端点部分和稳定子和乐部分；将循环旋转诠释为嵌入空间单位球面上的平行输运，使Wilson loop类比成为严格定理；对检测器证明精确恒等式，将残余统计量与完整幸存的种子窗口位置数关联，并推导独立编辑下的衰减律。

Result: 证明残余统计量与完好种子窗口位置数成正比，推出衰减律ρ^(h+1)；并验证到三位小数：相同保留率下，幸存信号可分别为原信号的一半、四分之一或恰好为零，仅取决于编辑落点。

Conclusion: 端点语义相似度不足以预测水印退化，与乐相关的路径结构以及编辑位置的空间分布在决定水印存活性方面具有同等甚至更关键的作用。

Abstract: Statistical watermarks for language models live in the freedom of the signifier: they choose among tokens that are nearly equivalent in meaning, and they are therefore eroded by exactly those transformations which move the form of a text while leaving its content in place. The literature measures such transformations by their endpoint, through the semantic similarity between the original and the rewritten text. We show that the endpoint is the wrong statistic. Adapting the formalism of linguistic loops, we prove that the invariant of a chain of meaning-preserving transformations factorises canonically into an endpoint part and a holonomy in the stabiliser of the initial state, the second of which the semantic deficit cannot see; the loop rotation is parallel transport on the unit sphere of the embedding space, so that the analogy with the Wilson loop becomes a theorem rather than a figure of speech. On the side of the detector we prove an exact identity: the residual statistic is proportional to the number of positions whose seeding window survived intact, from which the decay law $ρ^{h+1}$ follows as the independent-edit corollary. The identity has a disconcerting consequence, which we confirm to three decimal places: at one and the same retention rate the surviving signal may be one half of the original, one quarter of it, or exactly nothing, according only to where the edits fall.

</details>


### [80] [Are LLMs becoming similarly creative? Evidence from three years of models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19437)
*Nirav Patel,Josiah Crossman,Eva Aggarwal,Emily Wenger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对三年来发布的大语言模型在开放式任务上的输出进行了初步分析，利用句子嵌入相似度衡量模型在Infinity-Chat100（真实世界开放式用户查询集）和Alternate Uses Task（心理测量学创造力评估）上的表现。研究发现模型输出的多样性随时间呈现统计学显著下降，表明LLM输出在创造性内容上趋于趋同，这可能对人类-AI协同创造性工作中的人类自主性构成威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多追踪LLM在具有可验证答案的任务上的表现，但对开放式任务（如创造力、原创性和多样性）中LLM表现如何演变知之甚少。随着LLM越来越广泛地支持人类构思和创造性工作，理解LLM在开放式任务上的性能趋势至关重要。

Method: 收集跨越三年模型发布的LLM输出，使用两个基准：Infinity-Chat100（真实世界的开放式用户查询集合）和Alternate Uses Task（成熟的创造力心理测量评估）；采用句子嵌入相似度来衡量LLM应答的多样性和趋同性趋势，并进行统计分析验证显著性。

Result: 研究发现模型输出多样性随时间呈现统计学显著下降，表明不同模型的LLM输出在创造性实质内容上正在趋向收敛。

Conclusion: 如果这种趋同趋势持续下去，LLM驱动的同质化可能逐步削弱人类在人类-AI协同创造性工作中的自主性，需要谨慎审视LLM在人类创造性过程中的角色。

Abstract: Many benchmarks track Large Language Model (LLM) performance on tasks with verifiable answers, but less is known about how LLM performance is evolving on open-ended tasks, where creativity, originality and diversity may matter as much as quality. As LLMs increasingly support human ideation and creative work, understanding trends in LLM performance on open-ended tasks is critical. This paper presents a preliminary analysis of LLM creative outputs spanning three years of model releases, examining model responses to Infinity-Chat100, a real-world collection of open-ended user queries, and the Alternate Uses Task, an established psychometric creativity assessment. Using sentence-embedding similarity, we examine trends in LLM responses to these prompts. Our findings show a statistically significant decrease in model output diversity over time, suggesting that LLM outputs may be converging in creative substance across models. If this trend persists, LLM-driven homogenization may progressively diminish human agency in human-AI co-creative work, demanding careful consideration of LLMs' role in the human creative process.

</details>


### [81] [SynFlow: A Multidimensional Diachronic Semantic Analysis Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19472)
*Bach Phan-Tat,Kris Heylen,Dirk Geeraerts,Stefano De Pascale,Dirk Speelman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SynFlow，一个用于语言使用多维历时分析的开源工具包。它统一了基于依赖共现、形态特征、构式配置和外部派生表示（如框架语义）的工作流程，支持多种距离度量、值级分解、统计检验和词汇填充的增量聚类。通过德语形容词viral的定性案例研究和SemEval-2020 Task 1的基准结果验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的词汇语义变化（LSC）建模主要依赖向量空间表示，虽然能检测变化但难以解释具体哪些使用维度发生了变化。另一方面，历时语料研究虽然能考察句法行为、形态、构式模式等可解释维度，但通常通过分离的分析流程进行，缺乏统一框架。作者希望弥合这两种方法之间的鸿沟。

Method: SynFlow将语言观测转换为时期特定的分布，并对多种表示类型（基于依赖的共现、形态特征、构式配置、外部派生表示如框架语义）应用统一的工作流程。它支持不同的距离度量，以及值级分解、统计检验和词汇填充的增量聚类。

Result: 通过德语形容词viral的定性案例研究，展示了单一语义发展如何反映在句法、词汇、构式和形态等多个维度上。此外，报告了在SemEval-2020 Task 1上的已发表结果，用于定位这些表示相对于现有词汇语义变化检测系统的性能。

Conclusion: SynFlow为历时语言学分析提供了一个统一、可解释且多维度的工具包，使研究者能够在保留解释深度的同时进行词汇语义变化检测。

Abstract: Lexical semantic change (LSC) is commonly modelled through vector-space representations, but these approaches often provide limited insight into which aspects of usage are changing. Diachronic corpus research instead examines interpretable dimensions such as syntactic behaviour, morphology, and constructional patterns, but typically through separate analytical workflows. We present SynFlow, an open-source toolkit for multidimensional diachronic analysis of linguistic usage. SynFlow converts linguistic observations into period-specific distributions and applies a shared workflow across dependency-based co-occurrences, morphological features, constructional configurations, and externally derived representations such as Frame Semantics. It supports different distance measures, together with value-level decomposition, statistical testing, and incremental clustering of lexical fillers. We demonstrate SynFlow through a qualitative case study of the German adjective viral, showing how a single semantic development is reflected across syntactic, lexical, constructional, and morphological dimensions. We further report previously published results on SemEval-2020 Task 1 to situate the performance of these representations relative to existing lexical semantic change detection systems.

</details>


### [82] [Hear2Act: Benchmarking When Prosody Should Change What an Assistant Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19515)
*Xinyi Liu,Hooshang Nayyeri,Dilek Hakkani-Tur,Emine Yilmaz,JK Kim,Yifei Zhang,Charith Peris,Hari Thadakamalla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Hear2Act是一个统一评估协议，包含480个基于人格的场景、隐藏用户关切和可验证结果，用于测试语音助手是否能将韵律线索转化为下游任务决策。实验发现，音频大语言模型能从语音中恢复信息，但需显式中间表征才能真正改善行动决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常孤立地评估韵律感知、回应适当性和任务导向对话，难以测试韵律证据是否改变下游决策。需要一个统一的评估协议来衡量韵律线索对任务导向对话最终结果的影响。

Method: 构建Hear2Act，包含480个基于人格的场景，在保持任务和用户需求固定的前提下，同一关切分别通过显式词语和主要通过韵律传达，并在转录文本、音频和关切状态三种访问条件下评估决策。使用两个具备音频能力的大语言模型进行评估。

Result: 在韵律介导反馈下，将音频加入转录文本仅使平均最优解率从14.6%提升至15.3%；但当模型从音频推断关切状态、用文本表征并用于下一步行动选择时，最优解率升至39.6%，接近使用真实状态的40.7%。在显式词法反馈下，这种差异基本消失。

Conclusion: 韵律在词汇证据不足时至关重要；具备音频能力的大语言模型能从语音中恢复信息，但没有显式的中间表征时无法可靠地将韵律信息转化为行动决策。

Abstract: Prosodic cues can convey task-relevant information that alters the trajectory and outcome of a task-oriented dialogue, even when the words themselves remain unchanged. Yet existing benchmarks typically evaluate prosodic perception, response appropriateness, and task-oriented dialogue in isolation, making it difficult to test whether prosodic evidence changes downstream decisions. We introduce Hear2Act, a unified evaluation protocol for text and spoken assistants with 480 persona-grounded scenarios, hidden user concerns, and objectively verifiable outcomes. For each scenario, we keep the task and user needs fixed while varying whether the same concern is conveyed explicitly in words or primarily through prosody, and evaluate decisions under transcript, audio, and concern-state access.
  Using Hear2Act, we evaluate two audio-capable LLMs. Under Prosody-mediated feedback, adding audio to the transcript changes the average optimal-solution rate only from 14.6% to 15.3%. In contrast, when models infer the concern status from audio, represent it in text, and use it for next-action selection, the rate rises to 39.6%, close to 40.7% with the ground-truth state. This contrast, however, largely disappears under Explicit lexical feedback, where the concern is verbally mentioned in the utterance. Together, these results show that prosody matters when lexical evidence is insufficient, and that audio-capable LLMs can recover information from speech but do not reliably carry it into action without an explicit intermediate representation.

</details>


### [83] [Automated Summarization of Financial News Using Large Language Models and Retrieval-Augmented Generation: An Early Empirical Study (Fall 2023)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19526)
*Pranav Chandaliya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该项目于2023年秋季在乔治华盛顿大学开展，构建了一个利用大语言模型自动完成金融新闻摘要与股票数据叙述化的流水线，涵盖10家大型公司的新闻、背景和股价数据，对比了Summarize Chains与RAG（FAISS）两种方法在三个开源模型上的表现，发现Falcon-7B配合Summarize Chains效果最佳，同时指出了RAG在小模型上导致的重复和幻觉问题，并构建了Streamlit可视化仪表板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融分析师和投资者面临海量新闻与有限时间的矛盾，人工阅读和综合数百篇公司相关文章不可行，而遗漏关键信息会直接损害投资决策。迫切需要探索LLM能否可靠地自动化这一过程。

Method: 构建端到端流水线：从News API抓取新闻文章、从Wikipedia获取公司背景、从Yahoo Finance获取股价数据；开发了将数值表格转换为自然语言叙述的模板以适配LLM；对比两种摘要方法（Summarize Chains和基于FAISS的RAG）在三个开源模型（Falcon-7B-Instruct、DistilBART-CNN-12-6、BART-Large-XSum）上的新闻摘要表现，并使用GPT（text-davinci-003）生成股票摘要；以Lead-3作为基线，采用ROUGE-1评估；构建Streamlit仪表板用于交互式股票可视化。

Result: Falcon-7B配合Summarize Chains在新闻摘要任务中表现最佳，能够准确且连贯地覆盖所有新闻事件；RAG虽然在理论上前景光明，但在Falcon上引发严重重复，在BART-Large上当k值较大时产生事实幻觉；两种基于LLM的方法在ROUGE-1指标上均优于简单的Lead-3基线。

Conclusion: 该研究表明LLM可以成为股票新闻摘要自动化中的实用工具，且Summarize Chains在小型开源模型上优于RAG。研究记录了RAG在小模型下的失败模式（尤其是幻觉问题），这些发现对今天的RAG金融工具仍具参考价值。

Abstract: Stock market analysts and investors face a daily challenge: too much financial news, too little time. Manually reading and synthesizing hundreds of company-specific articles is impractical, yet missing key information can directly affect investment decisions. This project, conducted at George Washington University in Fall 2023, explores whether Large Language Models can automate this process reliably. We built a pipeline that pulls news articles from the News API, company background from Wikipedia, and stock price data from Yahoo Finance for ten major companies (AAPL, MSFT, GOOGL, AMZN, META, TSLA, JPM, NVDA, WMT, DIS). Because LLMs cannot directly process numerical tables, we developed a simple but effective template that converts stock data into natural language narratives. We then tested two summarization approaches (Summarize Chains and Retrieval-Augmented Generation with FAISS) across three open-source models (Falcon-7B-Instruct, DistilBART-CNN-12-6, BART-Large-XSum) for news, and GPT (text-davinci-003) for stock summaries. Falcon-7B with Summarize Chains gave the best results, covering all news events accurately and coherently. RAG, while promising in theory, caused severe repetition in Falcon and hallucinated facts in BART-Large when k was large. Both LLM-based approaches outperformed a simple Lead-3 baseline on ROUGE-1. We also built a Streamlit dashboard for interactive stock visualization. The work was done in Fall 2023, before RAG-based financial tools became widespread, and the failure modes we document, particularly hallucination under RAG in smaller models, remain relevant today.

</details>


### [84] [When Machines Speak: A Unified Generative Framework for Integrating Machine-Native Symbols into Pretrained Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19529)
*Su Yan,Rakesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniLang是一个统一的生成式框架，通过扩展预训练大语言模型的词表与嵌入空间，将机器原生符号视为与自然语言同等地位的一等生成单元，从而弥合语言建模与结构化预测之间的鸿沟。在序列推荐和法律先例预测两个任务上，UniLang均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的AI系统常以离散的机器原生符号（而非自然语言）表示实体、行为和结构化信息，这些符号位于预训练LLM的token空间之外，导致语言建模与结构化预测之间存在根本性割裂。

Method: UniLang通过扩展LLM的词表和嵌入空间，引入有grounding的机器原生表示，使文本token与符号token能够在单一自回归目标下被联合建模与生成，无需将符号翻译成自然语言或依赖任务特定架构。

Result: 在序列推荐和法律先例预测两个结构不同的任务上，UniLang一致性地优于多个强基线模型。

Conclusion: UniLang展示了将预训练LLM扩展到自然语言之外的可能性，使其能够作为异构机器原生表示的通用生成建模骨干。

Abstract: Many real-world AI systems represent entities, behaviors, and structured information using discrete machine-native symbols rather than natural language. While these representations are compact and preserve task-relevant structure, they lie outside the linguistic token space of pretrained large language models (LLMs), creating a fundamental divide between language modeling and structured prediction. We introduce UniLang, a unified generative framework that bridges this divide by extending pretrained LLMs to treat machine-native symbols as first-class generative units alongside natural-language tokens. UniLang expands the LLM's vocabulary and embedding space with grounded machine-native representations, enabling textual and symbolic tokens to be jointly modeled and generated under a single autoregressive objective. This unified interface allows pretrained LLMs to directly operate on machine-native representations without requiring them to be verbalized as natural language or relying on task-specific architectures. We evaluate UniLang on two structurally distinct tasks, sequential recommendation and legal precedent prediction, spanning different domains and types of structured prediction. Across both tasks, UniLang consistently outperforms strong baselines, demonstrating a path toward extending pretrained LLMs beyond language and using them as a common generative modeling backbone for heterogeneous machine-native representations.

</details>


### [85] [Generating Diverse Personas for User Simulators to Test Interview Dialogue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19549)
*Mikio Nakano,Kazunori Komatani,Hironori Takeuchi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用大语言模型自动生成用户模拟器人设（persona）的方法，用于测试访谈对话系统。通过为生成的人设赋予与沟通风格相关的个性特质，旨在增加用户模拟器输出话语的多样性，实验证明该方法能显著提升用户模拟器生成话语的变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 访谈对话系统与传统任务型对话系统类似，使用真人进行测试需要大量人力和成本，因此用户模拟器测试成为更优选择。然而现有用户模拟器主要面向训练任务型对话系统设计，缺乏对模拟用户人设的关注；在开发阶段测试访谈对话系统需要模拟多种用户行为，而手动创建大量人设非常耗费人力。

Method: 提出一种基于大语言模型自动生成用户模拟器人设的方法。该方法在为对话系统生成人设的同时，还赋予人设与沟通风格相关的个性特质（如交流方式上的差异），从而提升用户模拟器在对话中呈现的沟通风格多样性。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法能够使用户模拟器生成的话语具有更大的变异性（variation），即输出话语的多样性显著提升。

Conclusion: 自动生成带个性特质的人设可以有效提升访谈对话系统测试中用户模拟器的话语多样性，减少人工创建人设的劳动成本，为访谈对话系统的开发测试提供了一种高效且多样化的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses the issue of the significant labor required to test interview dialogue systems. While interview dialogue systems are expected to be useful in various scenarios, like other dialogue systems, testing them with human users requires significant effort and cost. Therefore, testing with user simulators can be beneficial. Since most conventional user simulators have been primarily designed for training task-oriented dialogue systems, little attention has been paid to the personas of the simulated users. During development, testing interview dialogue systems requires simulating a wide range of user behaviors, but manually creating a large number of personas is labor-intensive. We propose a method that automatically generates personas for user simulators using a large language model. Furthermore, by assigning personality traits related to communication styles when generating personas, we aim to increase the diversity of communication styles in the user simulator. Experimental results show that the proposed method enables the user simulator to generate utterances with greater variation.

</details>


### [86] [Reliable Financial Named Entity Recognition under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19558)
*Zihao Zheng,Baichuan Li,Junyi Yao,Jiayu Long*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对金融命名实体识别（NER）在分布偏移下的置信度估计与选择性预测问题，构建了涵盖SEC文件、金融新闻和社交媒体（极端域外）的三级压力测试，评估了BERT标注器和LoRA微调的Qwen小模型，发现不同置信度信号在跨域时的排名会变化，并以弃权机制（abstention）将句级错误从34.3%降至2%以下，同时提出先在领域偏移源头检测、再应用预测级置信度门控的分阶段部署策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融AI系统的信息抽取器通常在单一文本语域上训练，但实际部署会面临文件、新闻和用户生成内容等多种输入分布。标准F1分数无法反映当输入分布变化时哪些预测仍可安全自动化，因此需要研究置信度估计和选择性预测来保障自动化安全性。

Method: 构建三个由易到难的域偏移压力测试（SEC文件、金融新闻、社交媒体），评估BERT标签器和LoRA微调的Qwen2.5-0.5B/1.5B模型；使用五种推理时置信度信号、三种训练种子和bootstrap置信区间，系统比较各信号的错误检测能力与校准质量。

Result: （1）置信度排序本身会随分布偏移而变化：全局输出概率是域内最强错误检测器但域外性能退化，而实体跨度概率和自一致性（self-consistency）更稳健；（2）自一致性无需后处理拟合即具有更好的校准性；（3）弃权机制在域内最高置信度40%的输入上将句子错误率从34.3%降至2%以下，在金融新闻上仍然有效，但在极端社交媒体偏移下无法恢复出足够大的干净子集。

Conclusion: 研究建议采用分阶段部署策略：先在系统上游检测严重分布偏移，再应用预测级别的置信度门控，以保障金融AI系统在跨域部署中的安全自动化。

Abstract: Financial AI systems often train information extractors on one textual register and deploy them across filings, news, and user-generated content, while standard F1 scores do not indicate which predictions remain safe to automate when the input distribution changes. We study confidence estimation and selective prediction for financial named entity recognition (NER) on a three-tier stress test spanning SEC filings, financial news, and general-topic social media as an extreme out-of-domain condition. We evaluate a BERT tagger and LoRA-tuned Qwen2.5-0.5B/1.5B models using five inference-time confidence signals, three training seeds, and bootstrap intervals. Confidence rankings themselves change under distribution shift: whole-output probability is the strongest in-domain error detector but deteriorates out of domain, whereas entity-span probability and self-consistency are more robust; self-consistency is also better calibrated without post-hoc fitting. Abstention reduces sentence error from 34.3% to below 2% on the highest-confidence 40% of in-domain inputs and remains useful on financial news, but recovers no usefully large clean subset under the extreme social-media shift. These results motivate a staged deployment strategy that detects severe distribution shift upstream before applying prediction-level confidence gating.

</details>


### [87] [Remember, Verify, or Ask? Cross-Family Evaluation of Memory Commitment in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19564)
*Baichuan Li,Junyi Yao,Zihao Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MCB基准，研究持久记忆系统在LLM代理中的"记忆-澄清边界"问题——即交互信息应当被持久化、仅用于当前上下文、重新验证还是向用户澄清。MCB包含140个主场景和70个对比场景，评估动作标签和结构化工具调用选择。实验显示模型在验证变化事实方面优于主动向用户澄清模糊性，且记忆评估必须同时测试声明决策和工具调用选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆可以个性化LLM代理，但不正确的持久更新会静默扭曲未来行为。因此需要明确划定"记忆-澄清边界"：哪些交互信息应该持久化存储，哪些只应在当前上下文中使用，哪些需要重新验证，哪些需要向用户澄清。现有研究缺乏系统评估LLM代理在这方面的能力的基准。

Method: 构建MCB（Memory-Clarification Boundary）基准，包含140个主场景（70开发+70保留）和70个对比场景。评估两个维度：动作标签分类和结构化工具调用选择。由两位非作者独立标注70个保留主场景和70个对比场景（协议率97.1%，Cohen's kappa=0.962），第三位盲审解决4处分歧。在Claude和Qwen模型上进行测试，并采用few-shot提示和策略提示（policy prompt）进行比较。

Result: 模型验证变化事实（freshness）的能力优于向用户提出澄清请求的能力。Bare Qwen在0/12澄清项上提问，但验证了12/18个freshness项。Few-shot提示将准确率从0.557提升至0.771（增量+0.214，p_H=0.002），但澄清召回率仅为0.333。策略提示将错误持久化从0.243降至0.100（p_H=0.038），但准确率提升不显著。标签-工具一致性：Claude各模型为57%，Qwen为23%；Qwen的准确率从0.557降至0.343（p_H=0.047）。

Conclusion: 记忆评估必须同时测试口头陈述的决策（stated decisions）和实际工具调用选择（tool-call choices），因为二者可能存在显著不一致（如Qwen仅23%的一致性）。few-shot提示虽能提高整体准确率，但对澄清召回率的提升有限；策略提示能减少错误持久化但不足以显著提升整体准确率。

Abstract: Persistent memory can personalize an LLM agent, but an incorrect durable update can silently distort future behavior. We study the memory-clarification boundary: whether interaction-derived information should be persisted, used only in the current context, re-verified, or clarified with the user. MCB contains 140 primary scenarios, split into 70 development and 70 held-out items, plus a separate 70-item contrast set. It evaluates both action labels and structured tool-call selection. Two non-authors independently label the 70 held-out primary and 70 contrast items (97.1% agreement, Cohen's kappa = 0.962); a blind third resolves four disagreements, replacing eight author labels by non-author majority. Across Claude and Qwen, models verify changing facts more reliably than they ask users to resolve ambiguity. Bare Qwen asks on 0/12 clarification items while verifying 12/18 freshness items. Few-shot prompting raises accuracy from 0.557 to 0.771 (paired delta = +0.214, Holm-adjusted exact McNemar p_H = 0.002), yet clarification recall remains 0.333. The policy prompt reduces erroneous persistence from 0.243 to 0.100 (p_H = 0.038), although its accuracy gain is not significant. Label-tool agreement is 57% for each Claude model and 23% for Qwen; Qwen accuracy falls from 0.557 to 0.343 (p_H = 0.047). Memory evaluation must test both stated decisions and tool-call choices.

</details>


### [88] [Forking Fast: Efficiently Estimating Uncertainty Dynamics in Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19611)
*Eric Bigelow,Amir Zur,Satchel Grant,Tal Haklay,Can Rager,Owen Lewis,Thomas McGrath,Jack Merullo,Ekdeep Singh Lubana,Atticus Geiger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种更高效的LLM推理链重采样分析方法，通过统计建模平滑噪声、近似高质量数据，从而显著降低采样成本，并揭示推理不确定性动态的稳定模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理是随机的，理解模型需要刻画其对同一问题可能产生的推理链分布（即不确定性）。现有的基于重采样的分析虽然能揭示推理链中哪些步骤决定了模型如何得出答案，但在每个token或句子级别重采样文本序列成本极高，严重限制了此类分析的实用性。

Method: 作者首先证明当重采样大量推理链时，不确定性动态会收敛到稳定模式，噪声主要来自采样而非LLM对每个token或推理步骤的敏感性。基于这一发现，开发了一个统计模型来对低样本的噪声rollout数据进行平滑处理，以更好地逼近高样本数据的表现。

Result: 不确定性动态在大量重采样下呈现收敛的稳定模式，且通过所提出的统计平滑模型，可以用显著更少的采样成本获得接近高样本数据的分析质量。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅使重采样分析在计算上更加高效可行，还回答了推理不确定性动态的正确统计模型是什么这一科学问题，表明采样噪声而非模型对单个token的敏感性主导了低样本观测中的不确定性表象。

Abstract: LLM reasoning is stochastic, and so understanding a model requires grappling with the distribution of reasoning chains that it might produce for a given question, i.e., its uncertainty. Resampling-based analyses characterize this distribution, revealing which steps of a rollout determine how the model arrives at its answer. However, a major limitation of these approaches is that resampling text sequences at every token or sentence in a reasoning chain is very costly. Our work strives to make resampling analysis more computationally efficient, while also shedding light on an important scientific question: what is the right statistical model for explaining uncertainty dynamics in text generation? We show that when resampling many reasoning chains, uncertainty dynamics converge to stable patterns, and noise is largely an artifact of sampling rather than an LLM's sensitivity to each individual token or reasoning step. We develop a statistical model for smoothing noisy low-sample rollout data to better approximate high-sample data, allowing us to significantly cut sampling costs.

</details>


### [89] [Mitigating Identity Essentialism in LLM Agents with Longitudinal Life Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19621)
*Hexi Wang,Yujia Zhou,Bangde Du,Weihang Su,Xinyuan Cao,Qingyi Pan,Qingyao Ai,Yueyue Wu,Min Zhang,Yiqun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LifeMem，一种结合结构化生活事件检索与代理特定参数化记忆的纵向记忆框架，旨在解决LLM社会模拟中智能体缺乏人类多样化的问题，实验表明其在响应分布、组内多样性及个体跨生命阶段变化等方面更接近真实人类数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM社会模拟方法虽能部分复现人口层面的模式，但生成的智能体缺乏人类般的多样性。静态档案智能体表现出比人类更强的群体分离和组内压缩现象，这源于身份本质主义——人口统计标签被模型当作个体特质，导致组内响应同质化。根本原因包括两点：智能体表示过于稀疏且静态，以及纯提示词记忆无法持久整合经验。

Method: 提出LifeMem框架，受互补记忆系统启发，包含两个核心组件：(1) 结构化生活事件检索，用于提取和利用个体的关键生活事件；(2) 代理特定的参数化记忆，用于持续整合经验并动态更新个体表征。在Add Health和Understanding Society两个数据集上，使用三个不同的LLM进行评估。

Result: 与静态档案方法相比，LifeMem在响应分布、整体及组内多样性、个体跨生命阶段响应变化模式等多个维度上均更接近真实人类数据。

Conclusion: 纵向生活事件记忆对于构建更忠实且动态演化的社会智能体具有重要价值，为LLM社会模拟中智能体构建提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer a scalable approach to social simulation, but their credibility depends on how agents are constructed. Existing methods can partially reproduce population-level patterns, yet often fail to capture human-like diversity. Our analysis shows that static-profile agents exhibit stronger demographic separation and within-group compression than humans, a pattern consistent with identity essentialism: demographic labels can encourage models to treat group-average tendencies as individual traits, homogenizing responses within groups. We argue that this limitation arises from two related factors: sparse, static agent representations and the limited ability of prompt-only memory to persistently integrate experience. Inspired by complementary memory systems, we propose LifeMem, a longitudinal memory framework that combines structured life-event retrieval with agent-specific parametric memory for experience integration. Experiments on Add Health and Understanding Society with three LLMs show that LifeMem improves alignment with human data in terms of response distributions, overall and within-group diversity, and patterns of within-person response change across life stages. These findings highlight the value of longitudinal life-event memory for constructing more faithful and dynamically evolving social agents.

</details>


### [90] [ReCache: Efficient KV Cache Reuse and Compression for Tool-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19662)
*Yichu Fang,Sitong Wei,Haozhe Hu,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReCache是一个针对智能体语言模型的框架，通过独立缓存资源表示来降低推理时的计算和内存开销。它采用资源级注意力（去掉跨资源交互、分配资源局部位置）生成组合无关的KV块，并通过层-注意力头组路由剪枝和结构化/语义剪枝来限制资源可见性并保留关键字段。在七个公开工具/技能数据集上，资源级注意力匹配密集调用的性能（82.3% vs 82.4% Inv-F1），同时实现3.655倍的首令牌时间加速；完整框架减少92.43%的KV张量内存分配并加速注意力1.423倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体语言模型在多次请求中以不同组合和顺序反复编码工具和技能模式，导致标准前缀缓存无法重用其KV状态，造成推理时的高计算和内存开销。现有缓存机制无法应对这种组合变化性的资源调用场景。

Method: ReCache包含三个核心组件：(1) 资源级注意力（resource-wise attention），消除跨资源交互并分配资源局部位置，生成组合不变的KV块以便独立缓存；(2) 基于贡献的层-注意力头组路由，限制资源可见性到关键路由；(3) 结构性和语义性剪枝，仅保留调用关键字段以进一步减少存储。

Result: 资源级注意力在Inv-F1上达到82.3%，与密集调用的82.4%几乎持平，同时提供3.655倍TTFT加速。完整ReCache框架将KV张量内存分配减少92.43%，注意力加速1.423倍。在七个公开工具/技能使用数据集（含资源不相交测试）上验证了效果。

Conclusion: 将可复用的模式编码与选择性资源访问相分离，可以显著降低智能体推理成本且仅有有限的效果损失。ReCache证明了这种解耦思路的有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Agentic language models repeatedly encode tool and skill schemas that recur across requests in different combinations and orders, preventing standard prefix caching from reusing their key--value (KV) states. We introduce \textbf{ReCache}, a framework for independently caching resource representations while reducing their inference-time computational and memory overhead. Resource-wise attention removes cross-resource interactions and assigns resource-local positions, producing composition-invariant KV blocks. ReCache then restricts resource visibility to contribution-selected layer--KV-head-group routes and retains only invocation-critical fields through structural and semantic pruning. We evaluate ReCache on a benchmark assembled from seven public tool- and skill-use datasets, including resource-disjoint tests. Resource-wise attention matches dense invocation performance (82.3\% versus 82.4\% Inv-F1) while providing a 3.655$\times$ time-to-first-token speedup. The complete framework reduces allocated KV-tensor memory by 92.43\% and accelerates attention by 1.423$\times$. These results show that separating reusable schema encoding from selective resource access substantially reduces agentic inference costs with limited effectiveness loss. The code is available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/ReCache.

</details>


### [91] [The Asymmetric Harms of LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19670)
*Yuan Wu,Mairui Li,Lesia Semenova,Chudi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了3种LLM在11种压缩方法下的表现，发现压缩会不对称地影响模型行为：头部知识保留下降多于尾部知识、模型对错误答案仍保持高置信度、聚合偏置分数掩盖了不同人口亚组间相反的刻板印象偏好变化，表明压缩存在聚合指标无法捕捉的不对称行为改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM压缩虽然能降低部署成本，但常用的聚合指标（如困惑度和准确率）往往掩盖了模型底层的行为变化，缺乏对压缩后模型在知识保留、置信度和社会偏见等方面细粒度影响的系统研究。

Method: 对3个LLM应用11种压缩方法，系统评估压缩对知识保留（区分头部/尾部知识）、模型置信度（错误答案上的置信水平）以及社会偏见（按人口亚组细分分析）三个方面的影响。

Result: 压缩不成比例地降低了头部知识的相对保留量（相比尾部知识）；压缩模型在新丢失的知识上仍保持较高的错误置信度；稳定的聚合偏置分数掩盖了不同人口亚组间在刻板印象偏好上的大幅且相反的变化。

Conclusion: 压缩引发的不对称行为变化无法被聚合性能指标捕获，部署压缩模型前需要进行细粒度的评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) compression reduces deployment costs, but standard aggregate metrics like perplexity and accuracy often mask underlying behavioral shifts. In this work, we systematically evaluate 3 LLMs across 11 compression methods to investigate the effects of compression on knowledge retention, model confidence, and social bias. We find that compression disproportionately reduces the relative retention of head knowledge compared to tail knowledge. Furthermore, compressed models often remain substantially confident in their incorrect answers on newly lost knowledge. Finally, we demonstrate that stable aggregate bias scores can conceal substantial, opposing shifts in stereotypical preferences across demographic subgroups. Together, these findings reveal asymmetric behavioral changes that aggregate performance measures fail to capture, highlighting the need for granular evaluation of compressed models before deployment.

</details>


### [92] [Projector Is All You Train](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19726)
*Nyx Iskandar,Saathvik Selvan,Slater Victoroff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究多模态大语言模型（MLLM）训练中是否必须微调语言模型主干。实验表明，仅训练投影器（projector）即可达到与联合训练相当的强多模态性能，同时避免语言模型已有能力的漂移，且训练吞吐量约提升两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的MLLM训练通常同时调整语言模型主干和投影器，但作者质疑微调主干是否必要，希望探索更轻量、更高效且不损害语言模型原有能力的训练范式。

Method: 在3D多模态大语言模型上进行对照实验，比较仅训练投影器（projector-only）与联合训练（joint training，同时微调主干和投影器）两种策略，使用相同的编码器和主干，评估不同语言模型主干下的3D分类、3D描述生成以及语言、视觉和空间推理基准性能。

Result: 仅训练投影器即可获得与现有基线模型和联合训练模型相当的强多模态性能；联合训练导致语言模型已有能力的漂移，而仅训练投影器天然避免该问题；仅训练投影器的训练样本吞吐量约为联合训练的两倍。

Conclusion: 对于将MLLM适应到新模态的任务，仅微调投影器是一种充分且更优的训练策略，既保持性能又保护语言模型原有能力，同时提升训练效率。

Abstract: The typical training process of a multimodal large language model (MLLM) involves adapting both the language model backbone and the projector between the backbone and a modality-specific encoder. We ask whether fine-tuning the backbone of an MLLM is necessary to adapt it to a new modality. Through experiments on 3D MLLMs, we find that training only the projector is sufficient to achieve strong multimodal performance relative to existing baseline models and our jointly trained MLLMs with the same encoder and backbone. We also show that joint training leads to undesirable drift in existing capabilities of the language model, which projector-only training avoids by definition. Furthermore, projector-only training has approximately twice the training sample throughput of joint training. We validate our findings across different language model backbones via 3D classification and captioning benchmarks as well as standard benchmarks evaluating language, vision, and spatial reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [93] [One Success Isn't Reliability: Thinkingbox, a Sandbox and Benchmark for Agents in Stateful Business Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19741)
*Zhuochun Li,Youngmin Ko,Ali Keramati,Nicola Ferri,Susana Palmaz Lopez Pelaez,Liang-Chun Tsai,Calvin Wang,Mirco Milletari,Tuhin Kundu,Vadim Smolyakov,Kjartan Olafsson,Tommy Guy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Thinkingbox，一个用于工具-代理-用户交互的沙盒环境，支持隔离的MCP兼容工具会话、完整执行轨迹和终端后端状态的结果评估。基于此构建的Thinkingbox-bench包含507个策略条件化工作流，涉及零售、酒店、汽车保险、新银行内部IT和咨询IT/HR支持等场景。实验显示最强模型pass@1为65.36%但pass^20仅25.25%，揭示了当前模型在可靠完成有状态业务任务方面存在巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理基准多评估"产生合理响应或有效工具调用"这一层面，但对于超越代码的实际业务工作，代理需要在多轮交互中收集缺失信息、遵循领域策略、协调依赖工具并实现正确的持久状态转换而不产生附带影响。响应或工具调用级别的信号无法作为端到端任务完成的代理指标，需要更严格的评估框架。

Method: 构建Thinkingbox沙盒，提供隔离的MCP兼容工具会话、完整执行轨迹和基于终端后端状态的出评评估；在此基础上构建包含507个策略条件化工作流的Thinkingbox-bench基准，覆盖多个业务场景，每个尝试通过任务特定的可执行检查评估，接受合法轨迹同时拒绝错误、缺失或多余的效果；部分任务还检查最终响应所需属性。

Result: 最强的封闭和开源模型pass@1达65.36%，但pass^20仅25.25%。许多失败尝试显示了干净终止和有效的状态改变动作，表明响应或工具调用级别信号不能清晰代理端到端任务完成情况。Thinkingbox-bench揭示了"偶尔找到成功轨迹"与"可靠完成有状态业务任务"之间的巨大差距。

Conclusion: 现有模型在端到端有状态业务任务上的可靠完成能力不足，需要新的评估范式和训练方法来弥合这一差距。Thinkingbox及其基准已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: Recent agent benchmarks increasingly ground evaluation in executable environments, from code repair to web navigation, app APIs, and function calling. Yet completing consequential work beyond code requires more than producing a plausible response or valid tool call: agents must gather missing information over multiple turns, follow domain policies, coordinate dependent tools, and realize the correct persistent state transition without collateral effects. In this paper, we introduce Thinkingbox, a sandbox for tool-agent-user interaction that provides isolated MCP-compatible tool sessions, complete execution traces, and outcome evaluation over terminal backend state. Built on this sandbox, Thinkingbox-bench contains 507 policy-conditioned workflows across numerous scenarios, including retail, hospitality, auto insurance, neobank internal IT, and consulting IT/HR support. Each attempt is evaluated by task-specific executable checks that accept valid trajectories while rejecting wrong, missing, or extra effects; designated tasks additionally check required properties of the final response. Across proprietary and open-weight models, the strongest achieves 65.36% pass@1, but only 25.25% pass^20. Moreover, many failed trials show clean termination and valid state-changing actions, showing that response or tool-call-level signals are not clear proxies for end-to-end task completion. Thinkingbox-bench reveals a large gap between occasionally finding a successful trajectory and reliably completing stateful business tasks. We release both Thinkingbox and Thinkingbox-Bench: https://github.com/microsoft/thinkingbox

</details>


### [94] [PersonalBench: Measuring the Authorship Gap in LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19746)
*Yash Ganpat Sawant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PersonalBench基准测试，通过LUAR作者验证模型、LLM-as-judge和自动文体测量三个独立维度评估推理时个性化生成方法。发现个性化方法确实能产生作者差异化输出（LUAR AUC=0.918），但始终无法跨越人机边界——所有方法对真实作者的相似度（0.484-0.508）远低于跨作者人类基线（0.626-0.756）。LLM自身作者指纹主导输出，生成文本与任何人类作者的距离都比随机人类之间更远。各方法在LUAR上统计上不可区分（差值仅0.024），且LUAR与LLM判断之间的差异源于特征提取与画像提取的循环性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化文本生成基准仅评估任务准确率或偏好对齐，未真正检验模型输出是否在风格上接近目标作者，缺乏一个校准的、多维度的作者身份相似度评估标准。

Method: 构建PersonalBench基准，包含50位作者、1000个生成样本、两个模型家族（Qwen 3和GLM-4），通过三个独立视角评估：LUAR（训练的作者验证模型）、LLM-as-judge、自动化文体测量，并验证LUAR在语料上的可靠性（单帖AUC=0.76，多帖=0.96）。

Result: 个性化方法产生作者可区分输出（AUC=0.918），但所有方法对真实作者的LUAR相似度仅0.484-0.508，低于人类跨作者下限0.626。各方法在LUAR上不可区分（差值0.024），而LLM判断显示差异化，差异源于特征提取和画像提取的循环性。

Conclusion: 推理时个性化方法只能调制LLM的风格，而无法弥合与人类作者身份之间的鸿沟；PersonalBench可作为校准的测量标尺，但现有方法本质上仍被LLM自身的作者指纹所主导。

Abstract: Personalized text generation aims to make LLMs write in a specific individual's style, yet existing benchmarks measure task accuracy or preference alignment rather than whether the model's output actually resembles the target author's writing. We introduce PersonalBench, a benchmark that evaluates inference-time personalization methods through three independent lenses: LUAR (a trained authorship verification model), an LLM-as-judge, and automated stylometrics. Across 50 authors, 1,000 generations, and two model families (Qwen 3, GLM-4), we find that personalization methods do produce author-differentiated output (LUAR discriminates target authors within generated text at AUC=0.918) but this differentiation never crosses the human-LLM boundary. All methods achieve LUAR similarity to real authors in the range 0.484-0.508, below the cross-author human floor of 0.626 (ceiling 0.756). The LLM's own authorship fingerprint dominates: generated text is more distant from any human author than random humans are from each other. Methods are statistically indistinguishable from each other on LUAR (spread 0.024) despite appearing differentiated on the LLM judge, a discrepancy we trace to circularity between trait extraction and profile extraction. We validate that LUAR reliably measures authorship in our corpus (AUC=0.76 single-post, 0.96 multi-post). We release PersonalBench as a calibrated measuring stick: inference-time personalization modulates the LLM's style but does not bridge the gap to human authorship.

</details>


### [95] [FlashPrefill V2: Block-Sparse Prefill Attention for Long-Context LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19758)
*Qihang Fan,Huaibo Huang,Zhiying Wu,Bingning Wang,Ran He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FlashPrefill V2将FlashPrefill从算法原型演进为实用的长上下文服务系统，通过均值校正项抑制近似误差、基于PackGQA/翘曲专业化/乒乓流水线重设计稀疏注意力算子以对齐FlashAttention-3/4并支持FP8推理，同时原生支持分页KV缓存和连续批处理，可在SGLang等推理框架中集成。在NVIDIA H20 GPU上，128K上下文下较FlashAttention-2获得最高47.26x（FP8）和27.19x（BF16）加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文建模是LLM的关键能力，但注意力机制的二次复杂度在计算密集的prefill阶段构成严重瓶颈。前作FlashPrefill虽通过瞬时模式发现和基于最大值的动态阈值化缓解了这一问题，但仍停留在算法原型层面，距生产部署有较大距离。

Method: 沿三个维度将FlashPrefill从原型推向实用长上下文服务：1）引入均值校正项有效抑制近似误差，即使在高稀疏度下也能控制性能退化；2）使用PackGQA内存访问、翘曲专业化（warp specialization）和乒乓流水线（pingpong pipelining）重新设计稀疏注意力算子，完全对齐最新FlashAttention-3/4实现并支持FP8推理；3）原生支持分页KV缓存和连续批处理，可作为注意力后端集成到SGLang等现代推理框架中。

Result: 在NVIDIA H20 GPU（应用最广泛的推理加速器之一）上的大量评估表明，128K上下文长度下，FlashPrefill V2较FlashAttention-2在FP8和BF16精度下分别获得最高47.26倍和27.19倍加速；在FP8下，较FA3/4对齐的密集基线仍获得30.49倍加速。

Conclusion: FlashPrefill V2成功将稀疏注意力加速方案从理论原型落地为可生产部署的长上下文服务方案，在极端稀疏度下保持性能可控，并满足实际推理框架的量化与系统集成需求，为长上下文LLM推理提供了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Long-context modeling is a pivotal capability for Large Language Models, yet the quadratic complexity of attention remains a critical bottleneck, particularly during the compute-intensive prefilling phase. Our previous work, FlashPrefill, mitigates this cost through instantaneous pattern discovery and max-based dynamic thresholding; however, it remains an algorithmic prototype that is still distant from production deployment. In this paper, we present FlashPrefill V2, which evolves FlashPrefill from a prototype toward practical long-context serving along three dimensions. First, we introduce a mean correction term that effectively suppresses the approximation error, keeping performance degradation manageable even at extreme sparsity levels. Second, we redesign the sparse attention operator with PackGQA memory access, warp specialization, and pingpong pipelining, fully aligning with the latest FlashAttention-3/4 implementations and supporting FP8 inference to meet practical quantization requirements. Third, FlashPrefill V2 natively supports paged KV cache and continuous batching, allowing integration as an attention backend in modern inference frameworks such as SGLang. Extensive evaluations on NVIDIA H20 GPUs---among the most widely deployed inference accelerators---demonstrate that FlashPrefill V2 delivers up to 47.26x and 27.19x speedups over FlashAttention-2 at 128K context length under FP8 and BF16 precision, respectively, and, in FP8, still achieves a 30.49x speedup against an FA3/4-aligned dense baseline.

</details>


### [96] [SWE-bench Science: Can Coding Agents Resolve Engineering Tasks in Science?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19799)
*Zhipeng Xu,Jiahao Lu,Yining Zheng,Yuxin Wang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-bench Science，一个涵盖98个GitHub仓库、119个任务、跨越20个科学领域的仓库级科学软件工程基准，用于评估编码代理修复科学软件的失败机制。实验表明即使最佳代理（Claude Code with Opus-5 max）的pass@1也低于50%，并揭示了四类反复出现的失败机制及科学知识在修复中的非均匀收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件日益成为科学仪器本身的一部分，科学代码的故障不仅影响程序行为，还会损害科学结论的证据基础。然而现有编码代理评估主要关注聚合任务成功率，无法深入理解代理在修复科学软件时失败的根本原因。缺乏针对科学软件工程场景的仓库级基准来系统研究编码代理的能力边界与失败机制。

Method: 构建SWE-bench Science基准，包含来自98个GitHub仓库的119个任务，覆盖20个科学领域，并将任务划分为三种范式：问题驱动（Issue-driven）、专家探索（Expert-exploratory）和工程集成（Engineering-integration）。通过大规模评估编码代理，识别四类失败机制，并进行配对消融实验，比较提供与移除显式科学知识引导下代理的修复表现与token效率。

Result: 最佳代理Claude Code with Opus-5 (max)的pass@1低于50%。识别出四类失败机制：科学知识或抽象能力不足、误导性探索或表面修复、修复覆盖不完整或系统集成不足、科学知识缺乏泛化。消融实验表明科学知识并非均匀有益：良好对齐的信息能约束修复空间并提升平均性能与token效率，而对齐不佳的引导会诱发锚定效应且不必然提升精确修复成功率。

Conclusion: SWE-bench Science为研究编码代理在科学软件工程中的能力与失败机制提供了广泛的测试平台。研究强调了科学知识在代码修复中的复杂作用，提示构建科学软件修复代理时需要审慎评估知识引导的对齐质量，而非简单地增加信息输入。

Abstract: Software increasingly functions as part of the scientific instrument itself, making failures in scientific code capable of compromising not only program behavior but also the evidence underlying scientific conclusions. Yet existing evaluations of coding agents largely emphasize aggregate task success, providing limited insight into why agents fail when repairing scientific software. We introduce \textbf{SWE-bench Science}, a repository-level benchmark for scientific software engineering comprising 119 tasks from 98 GitHub repositories across 20 scientific domains. Each task is organized into one of three paradigms: Issue-driven, Expert-exploratory, and Engineering-integration. Even the best-performing agent, \textbf{Claude Code with Opus-5 (max), achieves a pass@1 below 50\%}, highlighting the substantial challenges posed by scientific software engineering. We identify four recurring failure mechanisms: deficits in scientific knowledge or abstraction, misguided exploration or surface-level repair, incomplete repair coverage or system integration, and failures to generalize scientific knowledge beyond observed cases in our analysis. We further conduct a paired ablation that removes explicit scientific guidance while preserving the repository and executable engineering context. The results show that scientific knowledge is not uniformly beneficial: well-grounded information can constrain repair and improve average performance and token efficiency, whereas poorly aligned guidance can induce anchoring and does not necessarily improve exact repair success. Together, SWE-bench Science provides a broad testbed for studying both the capabilities and failure mechanisms of coding agents in scientific software engineering.

</details>


### [97] [LoRA-GA$^2$: Low Rank Adaptation with Multi-step Gradient Adaptive Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19800)
*Haonan He,Xinyue Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LoRA-GA²是一种基于多步梯度信息的LoRA改进算法，通过轻量级探针捕获预训练权重的多步梯度动态，结合频谱感知的秩分配和最优初始化，在GLUE、GSM8K和HumanEval等基准上显著超越现有LoRA变体，同时保持原始LoRA的效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA在降低内存开销的同时与全量微调之间存在持续的性能差距。现有研究通过一步梯度近似来缩小这一差距，但无法完整捕捉梯度的动态变化过程，因此需要更全面的多步梯度信息来更好地对齐LoRA更新与全量微调的内在方向。

Method: 提出LoRA-GA²算法：(1) 引入轻量级探针用于获取预训练权重的多步梯度信息，不增加GPU内存开销，仅有边际时间开销；(2) 基于多步梯度设计频谱感知的、基于重要性的秩分配策略；(3) 利用多步梯度推导最优初始化参数。

Result: 实验结果表明LoRA-GA²在多个基准上持续优于现有LoRA变体：GLUE基准上平均超越最强基线0.66分，GSM8K上超越1.03分，HumanEval上超越0.87分，同时保持原始LoRA的效率优势。

Conclusion: LoRA-GA²通过充分利用多步梯度信息有效缩小了LoRA与全量微调之间的性能差距，为LoRA算法的改进提供了新方向，在性能和效率之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a prominent fine-tuning method for large models, achieving competitive performance with reduced memory overhead. However, a persistent performance gap remains between LoRA and full fine-tuning. Recent studies have sought to narrow this gap by employing one-step gradient approximations of pretrained weights to align LoRA updates with the principal directions or intrinsic dimensionalities of full fine-tuning updates. Nevertheless, these approaches fail to capture the full dynamics of the gradients. In this paper, we propose LoRA-GA$^2$, an effective fine-tuning algorithm that fully leverages multi-step gradient information. Specifically, we introduce a lightweight probe for multi-step gradients of pretrained weights that incurs no additional GPU memory cost and only marginal time overhead. We further employ a spectrum-aware, importance-based rank allocation and optimal initialization derived from multi-step gradients. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that LoRA-GA$^2$ consistently outperforms existing LoRA variants while preserving the efficiency advantages of vanilla LoRA. For instance, LoRA-GA$^2$ surpasses the leading baseline by an average of 0.66 points on the GLUE benchmark, and outperforms the strongest baseline by 1.03 points on GSM8K and 0.87 points on HumanEval, respectively.

</details>


### [98] [Stopping and Routing LLM Judge Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19802)
*Bin Zhu,Yi Xie,Yanghui Rao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM评估中的裁判（judge）面板设计形式化为一个按角色（role-conditioned）进行资源分配的问题，通过小规模标注审计集和声明的数据切片，估计裁判的三种角色（冗余副本、全局互补者、切片专家），并据此生成可复用的裁判调用策略：丢弃冗余副本、全局添加互补者、按条件路由专家、在验证增益低于阈值时停止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评估管线中往往同时存在多种候选裁判（通用LLM裁判提示、奖励模型、安全分类器、置信度变体、任务特定验证器等），部署时面临的不仅是"哪个裁判最好"的问题，还包括哪些裁判应该被调用、在哪些样本上调用、以及何时停止构建裁判面板。现有方法未系统性地解决这个联合分配问题。

Method: 将裁判面板设计形式化为角色条件分配问题。从一个小型标注审计集、声明的数据切片和裁判成本出发，估计三种相对目标（target-relative）的角色：副本（copies）不增加条件信息、互补者（complements）能改进全局面板、专家（specialists）仅对特定切片有效。这些角色推导出相应策略：丢弃副本、全局添加互补者、按条件路由专家、当验证增益低于阈值时停止建面板。

Result: 在推理、代码、安全、偏好、奖励模型、摘要和数学等多个审计场景中，将方法与单裁判、平铺面板、匹配多样性启发式、全量调用堆叠、可靠性陪审团和节俭级联等方法进行比较，得出了裁判调用的机制图谱：在可部署切片上路由专家、在饱和验证器场景中停止、在风险收益值得时保留广泛集成、忽略条件副本。

Conclusion: 该方法的输出是一个可复用、可审计的裁判调用计划，可直接用于下一批评估批次，提供了一种结构化的裁判面板设计与部署决策框架。

Abstract: LLM evaluation pipelines often have many candidate judges: general LLM-as-a-judge prompts, reward models, safety classifiers, confidence variants, and task-specific verifiers. The deployment question is not only which judge is best, but which judges should be called, on which examples, and when panel construction should stop. We formulate judge-panel design as a role-conditioned allocation problem. From a small labeled audit set, declared slices, and judge costs, the method estimates target-relative roles: copies add no conditional information, complements improve the global panel, and specialists help only on slices. These roles induce a policy: drop copies, add complements globally, route specialists conditionally, and stop when validation gain falls below a threshold. Across reasoning, code, safety, preference, reward-model, summarization, and math audits, the method is compared with single judges, flat panels, matched diversity heuristics, full-call stacking, reliability juries, and frugal cascades. The result is a regime map for judge calls: route specialists on deployable slices, stop in saturated verifier regimes, keep broad ensembles when their risk benefit is worth the cost, and ignore conditional copies. The output is a reusable, auditable call plan for the next evaluation batch.

</details>


### [99] [A knowledge-guided agentic framework for mitigating patient-context ambiguity in health queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19875)
*Mahyar Abbasian,Saba A. Farahani,Arshia Ilaty,Hung Cao,Ramesh Jain,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个知识引导的智能体框架，用于在医疗聊天机器人最终回复生成之前缓解患者上下文缺失导致的歧义。框架解释初始查询、利用知识图谱构建假设集合、识别缺失的患者上下文变量并主动提出追问问题，然后将原始查询与获取的上下文合并为澄清提示输入下游语言模型。在诊断检索和饮食安全分类两个基准上的实验表明，该方法相比直接回答和单纯改写，显著提升了五个语言模型的准确率和马修斯相关系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者向医疗聊天机器人提交的查询往往过于简短且缺少患者特定信息（如症状、诊断、药物、过敏或饮食限制），虽然语言上清晰，但可能存在多个合理答案。语言模型直接回答可能依赖未经支持的假设，导致错误或不安全的回复。现有方法缺乏在最终生成前系统性地识别并获取缺失患者上下文信息的机制。

Method: 提出一个知识引导的智能体框架，位于患者和下游语言模型之间。框架流程包括：(1) 解释初始查询；(2) 利用任务特定的知识图谱构建一组合理的假设（假说）；(3) 识别区分这些假说所需的缺失患者上下文变量；(4) 提出针对性的追问问题；(5) 将原始查询与获取的上下文合并为澄清提示，输入下游模型。该框架在五个语言模型上，使用两个受控的歧义缓解基准进行评估：诊断检索（1,034个症状查询，临床相关信息被系统掩蔽）和饮食安全分类（487个查询，关键健康上下文被省略）。

Result: 在诊断检索任务中，与直接提示相比，框架在所有五个评估模型上的整体精确Top-1准确率至少提高57.1个百分点，选择性精确Recall@5至少提高77.7个百分点。在饮食安全分类中，框架在所有五个模型上均提高了准确率，并在其中四个模型上取得了最高的马修斯相关系数。

Conclusion: 知识引导的追问框架能有效缓解医疗聊天机器人面临的患者上下文歧义问题，通过主动获取缺失的患者信息，在不改动下游语言模型的前提下显著提升回答的准确性和安全性。

Abstract: Patients often submit short, underspecified queries to healthcare chatbots that lack the patient-specific information needed to determine an appropriate response. Although these queries may be linguistically clear, they can support multiple plausible answers depending on undisclosed factors such as symptoms, diagnoses, medications, allergies, or dietary restrictions. A language model answering such a query directly may therefore rely on unsupported assumptions about the patient. We introduce a knowledge-guided agentic framework for mitigating patient-context ambiguity before final response generation. The framework operates between the patient and an otherwise unchanged downstream language model. It interprets the initial query, uses a task-specific knowledge graph to construct a set of plausible hypotheses, identifies the missing patient-context variables needed to distinguish among them, and asks targeted follow-up questions. The original query and the acquired context are then combined into a clarified prompt for the downstream model. We evaluated the framework across five language models using two controlled ambiguity-mitigation benchmarks: diagnosis retrieval from 1,034 symptom queries with clinically relevant evidence systematically masked, and dietary-safety classification from 487 queries with decisive health context omitted. The framework was compared with direct answering of the underspecified query and with rephrasing the same query without acquiring new patient information. In diagnosis retrieval, it increased overall exact Top-1 accuracy by at least 57.1 percentage points and selective exact Recall@5 by at least 77.7 percentage points across the five evaluated models compared with direct prompting. In dietary-safety classification, it improved accuracy across all five models and achieved the highest Matthews correlation coefficient for four...

</details>


### [100] [Interrupting the Loop: Periodic Subject Changes Raise Judged Surprise and Connection in Base Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19893)
*Roberto I. Ono Filho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 这篇论文通过24种条件下的受控实验，拆解了一个受认知启发的长文本生成循环。研究发现该循环的大部分效果来自单一操作：在几百年词元的间隔中注入新主题（中断），同时抑制字面重复（习惯化）。在基于前提单元的窗口判断协议下，中断使"惊讶感"提升1.2-1.4分、"连接性"提升0.8分（相对仅习惯化）。但窗口判断器存在三个盲区：将实验者注入的句子误判为模型自身输出、固定轮换注入句会诱导模型在判断器视野之外重放先前片段（被误判为惊讶和连接）、局部增益无法组合为完整文档。此外，中断在具有验证器的在线装箱问题上将有效启发式数量提升3-4倍，但不提升最佳解质量。论文最终报告的是长文本生成的评估协议和简单干预的受控特征描述，而非创造力机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望理解一个受认知启发的生成循环中"新颖性"的来源，并考察LLM判断器在长文本流中实际能"看到"什么。核心动机包括：1) 拆解生成循环中各操作的真实贡献；2) 建立可重复、可验证的长文本评估协议；3) 揭示窗口式判断器的系统性盲区及其对评估结果的影响。

Method: 在三个基础模型上进行24种条件下的受控实验。核心操作包括"中断"（每几百个词元注入新主题）和"习惯化"（抑制字面重复）。评估采用仅基于生成窗口文本的判断，以"前提"为单位（n=10），判断器经可重复性测量，并与第二类判断器族及人类读者对照。同时进行预注册的新前提复制实验，以及在在线装箱问题（有验证器）上的下游验证。

Result: 1) 中断使惊讶感提升1.2-1.4分，连接性提升0.8分（相对仅习惯化）；2) 要求连续性的接续词有害；3) 裸段落分隔符对新文本无可检测增益；4) 重置上下文至少与保留上下文效果相当；5) 预注册复制确认主要对照差异；6) 判断器将实验者注入句误判为模型自身输出；7) 固定轮换注入句诱导模型在判断器视野外重放先前片段，且被评分惊讶和连接（周期150-300时65-80%的窗口）；8) 局部增益不组合，无任何条件产生完整文档；9) 在线装箱问题中中断使有效不同启发式增加3-4倍但不提升最优解质量。

Conclusion: 该研究对长文本生成提供了受控的评估协议和对简单干预（中断+习惯化）的特征化描述。其局限性明确：不以机制性的创造力阐释为目标，且明确承认干预的局部成功无法组合成整体文档。论文为长文本评估提供了可复用的方法框架，同时警示了窗口式LLM判断器的系统性幻觉（将外部注入内容归因于模型）和评估盲区。

Abstract: Where does the novelty a base language model produces with no task come from, and what can an LLM judge of a long stream actually see? We dismantle a cognitively inspired generation loop over 24 conditions on three base models. Most of its effect lives in one operation: a new subject injected every few hundred tokens (an interruption) into a stream whose literal repetition is damped (habituation). We judge windows of generated text only, with the premise as the unit (n=10) and a judge measured for repeatability, against a second judge family and against human readers. Under that protocol the interruption raises judged surprise by 1.2 to 1.4 points and connection by 0.8 over habituation alone. A connective that asks for continuity hurts; a bare paragraph break adds nothing detectable on fresh text; a reset context does at least as well as a kept one; and a pre-registered replication on new premises confirms the primary contrast. Three things the window judge could not see changed the first version of this study, and we think they are of general use. The judge scores the experimenter's injected sentence as the model's own. A fixed rotation of injected sentences makes the model replay its earlier segments from beyond the judge's horizon, and the judge scores the replay as surprise and connection (65-80% of post-interruption windows at periods 150-300). And the local gains do not compose: no arm produces an integrated document. The salience monitor, the in-loop judge, memory across interruptions and a judge-gated Review run with a gate that opens add nothing. On a problem with a verifier (online bin packing), the interruption multiplies valid, distinct candidate heuristics three- to fourfold without raising the quality of the best. We report an evaluation protocol for long generation and a controlled characterization of a simple intervention, not a mechanism of creativity.

</details>


### [101] [Learning how to Forget: Fine-tuning for Long-Context Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19920)
*Matthias Seeger,Zeyu Zhang,Vihang Patil,Konstantinos Benidis,Sebastian Schelter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对KV缓存策略的稀疏注意力微调新方法，可在单张A100 GPU（40GB）上运行，让模型与任意KV缓存策略协同适应，通常优于使用精确注意力的模型。同时开源了KeysAndValues库，提供高性能的稀疏注意力实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长上下文Transformer推理在硬件预算有限时面临KV缓存内存爆炸的挑战，已有工作通过稀疏注意力进行KV缓存选择和压缩，但缺乏有效的方法让模型与这些KV缓存策略协同适应，且通常需要昂贵的精调资源（如序列并行）。

Method: 提出一种新的稀疏注意力微调方法，适用于任意KV缓存策略，在中等硬件预算（单块A100 40GB）下运行，允许模型与KV缓存策略共同适应。还提供了H2O稀疏注意力的高效实现，包含专门的缩放点积注意力（SDPA）内核支持。

Result: 该方法在中等硬件预算下训练，通常优于使用精确注意力（序列并行）训练的模型，并开源了KeysAndValues库（https://github.com/awslabs/keys_values），提供易用且高性能的代码。

Conclusion: 这项工作验证了模型与KV缓存策略协同微调的有效性，为长上下文推理提供了低硬件门槛的新方案，并通过开源库降低了复现和使用门槛。

Abstract: A lot of prior work addressed key-value (KV) cache selection and compression by sparse attention to enable long-context inference for transformer language models without excessive hardware budgets. We provide a new method for fine-tuning models with sparse attention. It works for any KV cache policy, runs on a moderate hardware budget (e.g., a single Nvidia A100 GPU with 40 GB RAM), and allows the model to co-adapt with the policy, often outperforming models trained with exact attention (sequence parallelism). We also provide an efficient implementation of H2O sparse attention (the leading policy in our experiments) with dedicated scaled dot product attention kernel support. KeysAndValues (https://github.com/awslabs/keys_values), a new open source library for long-context inference and fine-tuning, provides easy-to-use and performant code for all methods discussed here.

</details>


### [102] [Dynamic Gated Cross-Modal Fusion with Sarcastic-aware Contrastive Regularization for Multimodal Sarcasm Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19942)
*Hao Guo,Subin Huang,Junjie Chen,Zhifa Geng,Sanmin Liu,Chao Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出结合动态门控跨模态融合与讽刺感知对比正则化（SaCR）的多模态讽刺检测框架，通过实例级别的模态贡献校准和标签感知的对比学习，在MMSD和MMSD2.0数据集上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态讽刺检测中，现有方法依赖固定融合策略并将讽刺视为通用跨模态不匹配，难以捕捉实例依赖的模态贡献和误导性的语义一致性——即表面对齐掩盖了底层矛盾意图的情况。

Method: 提出MSD框架，包含三个关键组件：1) 双向门控交互模块实现跨模态特征过滤并自适应校准文本和视觉的实例级贡献；2) 动态融合门平衡模态重要性以生成鲁棒的多模态表示；3) SaCR标签感知对比正则化，对非讽刺样本鼓励语义一致性、对讽刺样本抑制误导性一致性。采用多目标学习策略联合优化多模态分类和辅助单模态监督。

Result: 在MMSD和MMSD2.0两个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法持续优于强基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过动态模态融合和讽刺感知的对比正则化，所提出的框架能有效解决实例依赖的模态贡献问题与误导性语义一致性挑战，显著提升多模态讽刺检测的准确性。

Abstract: Multimodal sarcasm detection aims to identify sarcastic intent from multimodal content, where inconsistencies between literal meaning and contextual cues often signal irony. This task has attracted increasing research attention. However, accurate detection remains challenging due to instance-dependent modality contributions and misleading semantic consistency, where surface-level alignment masks underlying contradictory intent. Existing methods often rely on fixed fusion strategies and treat sarcasm as generic cross-modal mismatch, limiting their ability to capture subtle sarcasm cues and instance-specific modality interactions. To address these challenges, we propose a novel MSD framework that integrates Dynamic Gated Cross-Modal Fusion with Sarcastic-aware Contrastive Regularization (SaCR). Specifically, a bidirectional gated interaction module performs cross-modal feature filtering and adaptively calibrates textual and visual contributions at the instance level. A dynamic fusion gate further balances modality importance to generate more robust multimodal representations. Furthermore, SaCR is introduced as a label-aware contrastive regularization objective that encourages semantic consistency for non-sarcastic samples while suppressing misleading consistency in sarcastic cases. The proposed framework is trained end-to-end with a multi-objective learning strategy that jointly optimizes multimodal classification and auxiliary unimodal supervision. Extensive experiments on MMSD and MMSD2.0 demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms strong baselines.

</details>


### [103] [Natural Language Code Retrieval for 1C:Enterprise: An Open Benchmark and Efficient Bi-Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19957)
*Konstantin Chesnokov,Chingiz Mingazov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对1C:Enterprise生态系统中自然语言代码检索缺乏开放数据集和专用模型的问题，提出了完整的解决方案：包含3,413个真实且脱敏的查询-代码对的开放基准、可复现的评估框架，以及专用双编码器模型。通过使用Matryoshka表示学习（MRL）和隐私感知分词器，在Google Gemma生成的784,057个合成三元组上微调模型，在平衡宏nDCG@10上达到0.5992，显著优于基线架构（0.4932）和google/embeddinggemma-300m（0.5404）。MRL截断至256维可保持99.9%的检索质量，同时将密集索引存储和精确相似度计算量减少三倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1C:Enterprise生态系统中，代码检索任务面临双重挑战：使用俄语语法且包含高度领域特定术语，导致现有方法和模型无法直接适用。同时，该领域缺乏开放数据集和专用模型，阻碍了研究进展。

Method: 本文提出完整的1C代码检索流水线：（1）构建包含3,413个经个人信息清洗的真实查询-代码对基准数据集；（2）开发可复现的评估工具链；（3）利用google/gemma-4-26B-A4B-it从公开代码库生成784,057个合成训练三元组，配合Matryoshka表示学习（MRL）和隐私感知分词器微调专用双编码器；由于基准子集大小不均，采用平衡子集宏、查询加权微和仅论坛三种评估指标。

Result: 模型在平衡宏nDCG@10上达到0.5992，微平均为0.5044，论坛子集为0.4617。对比基线的0.4932宏分数和google/embeddinggemma-300m的0.5404，均有显著提升。保守的13-gram重叠审计后性能仍保持0.6011（宏）/0.5010（微），证明训练-基准重叠不影响主要结论。MRL截断至256维时，保持99.9%检索质量，存储和精确相似度计算量减少三倍。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了1C:Enterprise代码检索领域的空白，证明了使用合成数据、MRL和隐私感知分词器的组合方法能有效克服标注数据稀缺问题，同时验证了模型性能的真实性（不受数据重叠影响）以及MRL在保持检索质量前提下的存储效率优势。

Abstract: Natural language code retrieval is a rapidly evolving task in computer science. However, the 1C:Enterprise ecosystem combines Russian syntax with highly domain-specific terminology, for which open datasets and specialized models have been virtually non-existent. We present a comprehensive pipeline for 1C code retrieval: an open benchmark of 3,413 real-world, PII-scrubbed query-code pairs, a reproducible evaluation harness, and a specialized bi-encoder. To overcome scarce labeled data, we fine-tune on 784,057 synthetic triplets generated by google/gemma-4-26B-A4B-it from public code repositories, using Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) and a privacy-aware tokenizer. Because the benchmark subsets differ in size, we report balanced-subset macro, query-weighted micro, and forum-only results. Our model reaches 0.5992 balanced macro nDCG@10, 0.5044 micro, and 0.4617 on forum, versus 0.4932 macro for the baseline architecture and 0.5404 for google/embeddinggemma-300m. Removing every benchmark example flagged by the conservative exact/13-gram overlap audit leaves 0.6011 balanced macro (0.5010 micro), indicating that detected train-benchmark overlap does not explain the headline result. MRL truncation to 256 dimensions preserves 99.9% of retrieval quality while reducing dense-index storage and exact similarity arithmetic by a factor of three.

</details>


### [104] [Robust Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis via Iterative Proxy Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19971)
*Zhifa Geng,Subin Huang,Hao Guo,Junjie Chen,Sanmin Liu,Chao Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种迭代式代理校正框架，用于多模态情感分析中模态缺失的情况。该方法通过门控残差校正逐步优化从非语言模态构建的语言代理，并根据语言可靠性评分自适应融合，在MOSI、MOSEI和SIMS数据集上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的多模态输入常不完整或被损坏，现有基于代理的方法采用一次性的代理构建来补偿语言信息的退化，但生成的代理初始化时可能粗糙或不可靠，过早注入会导致误差传播并损害情感预测。

Method: 1) 从非语言模态构建语言导向的代理；2) 在多模态上下文下通过门控残差校正逐步优化代理；3) 根据估计的语言可靠性得分，将校正后的代理与观测到的语言表示自适应融合；4) 引入阶段式潜变量校正目标，利用完整的语言表示作为训练时的语义锚点来稳定代理优化轨迹。

Result: 在MOSI、MOSEI和SIMS数据集上的广泛实验表明，在各种缺失模态设置下，该框架始终优于竞争性基线，实现了对不完整输入的稳健情感预测。

Conclusion: 迭代式代理校正框架有效解决了现有一次性代理方法在初始化不可靠、误差传播方面的局限性，通过渐进式优化和自适应融合提升了不完整多模态情感分析的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis aims to infer affective states by integrating language, visual, and acoustic cues. However, real-world multimodal inputs are often incomplete or corrupted, which can weaken cross-modal complementarity and introduce misleading information into downstream fusion. Existing proxy-based methods for incomplete MSA commonly rely on one-shot proxy construction to compensate for degraded language information, but the generated proxy may be coarse or unreliable at initialization. Prematurely injecting such a proxy into multimodal reasoning can propagate initial errors and compromise sentiment prediction. To address this limitation, we propose an iterative proxy correction framework for robust incomplete MSA. Our method constructs a language-oriented proxy from non-language modalities and progressively refines it under multimodal context through gated residual correction. The corrected proxy is then adaptively fused with the observed language representation according to an estimated language reliability score, allowing the model to balance proxy-based compensation and trustworthy linguistic evidence. In addition, we introduce a stage-wise latent correction objective that uses the complete language representation as a training-time semantic anchor to stabilize the proxy refinement trajectory. Extensive experiments on MOSI, MOSEI, and SIMS under diverse missing-modality settings demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms competitive baselines and achieves robust sentiment prediction under incomplete inputs.

</details>


### [105] [HealMed: Multilingual Evaluation of Large Language Models in Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19981)
*Yingjian Chen,Fan Gao,Sherry T. Tong,Haoyu Zhang,Aosong Feng,Kevin W. Jin,Xing Wu,Jinghui Lu,Abdul Samad,Akbar Faruqi,Cesar Caraballo,Cibele Brandão,Dhruva,Gupta,Eunji Jeon,Gabriel Madera-Santiago,Geon Lee,Hugo Toshio Itikawa,Insook Cho,Isabelli Martins,Isarar Siddique,Israr Ahmed,Jihyo Kwak,Kanyakorn Veerakanjana,Luis Guilherme Cardoso,Minjin Kim,Piyalitt Ittichaiwong,Renee Dua,Santiago Gudiño-Rosales,Xiujie Chen,Zeo Lapalus,Zixin Xu,Michihiro Yasunaga,Rex Ying,Heuiseok Lim,Jaewoo Kang,Chanjun Park,Hang Jiang,Ethan Goh,Hyunjae Kim,Edison Marrese-Taylor,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Qingyu Chen,Irene Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HealMed是一个由23位医学专家耗时两年构建的多语言医学评测基准，涵盖9种语言、9个数据集和3种任务格式，共9,000个示例。研究发现低资源语言上的模型性能下降最明显，专有模型跨语言稳定性最佳，而医学专业化的开源模型并不能保证多语言鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学领域的大语言模型评测基准缺乏多语言覆盖，导致跨语言性能差异难以被系统评估；翻译质量对评测结果的影响也未被充分量化。

Method: 构建包含9种语言、9个数据集、3种任务格式（MCQA、NLI、开放式问答）的基准，每个示例经两位精通英语和目标语言的专家独立评估与修订，并由分布在全球9个国家和地区的23名医生及医学专家共同开发。

Result: 低资源语言上的性能下降最显著但不同语言和模型间差距差异大；最强专有模型跨语言最稳定，开源和医学专用模型差距更大且不一致；专家修订既可能提升也可能降低性能分数，说明翻译质量对跨语言评测有实质影响。

Conclusion: 医学专业本身不能保证多语言鲁棒性，翻译质量是影响跨语言医学评测结果的关键因素，未来需要更严谨的多语言医学评测方法。

Abstract: We present HealMed, an expert-reviewed benchmark for multilingual evaluation of large language models in medicine. HealMed contains 1,000 examples in each of nine languages, drawn from nine datasets and covering three task formats: MCQA, NLI and open-ended QA. The benchmark was developed over two years by 23 physicians and medical experts based across nine countries and regions. Each translation was evaluated and revised by two experts fluent in English and the corresponding target language. On HealMed, performance declined most in low-resource languages, although the size of the gap varied markedly across languages and models. The strongest proprietary models were the most stable across languages, whereas many open-source and medically specialized models showed larger and less consistent gaps. Medical specialization alone did not ensure multilingual robustness. Furthermore, expert revision could either raise or lower measured performance, indicating that translation quality materially affects cross-language evaluation results.

</details>


### [106] [Auditing Cross-Lingual Fairness in Language Model Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20047)
*Alexander Nemecek,Osama Zafar,Debargha Ganguly,Vikash Singh,Vipin Chaudhary,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多语言水印评估框架，包含经验校准的检测阈值、阈值无关的伴随测量、三种质量测量范式和广义熵分解，系统评估了六种水印方案在十一门语言（跨越四种文字和八个语系）上的表现，发现跨语言差异主要来自语系之间而非个别语言，表明水印的跨语言公平性差距是结构性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印方案的评估几乎完全基于英语，使用统一的检测阈值和狭窄的质量指标。多语言部署会暴露许多在英语上无关紧要但决定跨语言结论的评估设计选择，因此需要一套专门针对多语言场景的评估框架。

Method: 提出四组件评估框架：(1) 按部署场景经验校准检测阈值；(2) 阈值无关的伴随测量以区分校准失败与检测失败；(3) 三种互不重叠的质量测量范式：分布性、配对语义、参考困惑度；(4) 在类型学语系划分上对跨语言差异进行广义熵分解。应用于六种水印方案、三个开源模型、十一门语言（四文字、八语系）、基座与指令微调两种模式。

Result: 单语言单范式的评估无法暴露的失败模式在该框架下得以显现。在检测和质量两个维度上，观测到的差异主要在类型学语系之间而非语系内部，表明水印的跨语言公平性差距源自语言的结构属性而非特定语言的特异性。

Conclusion: 跨语言水印评估需要考虑语言类型学结构，单一语言、单一范式的评估方式会掩盖关键失败模式；水印方案在多语言场景下的公平性差距是结构性的，与语言类型学属性密切相关。

Abstract: Watermarking schemes for large language model output are evaluated almost exclusively on English text using each scheme's detection threshold and a narrow set of quality measurements. Multilingual deployment exposes evaluation-design choices that are inconsequential on English but determine conclusions cross-lingually. We propose an evaluation framework with four components: detection thresholds calibrated empirically per deployment context, a threshold-independent companion measurement that distinguishes calibration failures from detection failures, three disjoint quality measurement paradigms (distributional, paired-semantic, and reference-perplexity), and a generalized-entropy decomposition of cross-language disparity over a typological family partition. Applied to six watermarking schemes, three open-weight generators, eleven languages spanning four scripts and eight typological families, and both base and instruction-tuned regimes, the framework reveals failure modes that single-language single-paradigm evaluation cannot surface. Across detection and quality, observed disparity is predominantly between-family on the typological partition, indicating that cross-lingual fairness gaps in watermarking are structural to language properties rather than idiosyncratic to particular languages.

</details>


### [107] [SABET-QA: Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20083)
*Brahim Touayouch,Mirette Moawad,Dmitry Akulov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SABET-QA框架，通过双向实体-时间评分机制和槽感知上下文模块，在多跳推理中迭代细化推理状态，解决嵌入方法在时序知识图谱问答中单次推理无法处理多步查询的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于嵌入的时序知识图谱问答方法采用单次推理流水线，难以处理需要跨多步推理的时间敏感查询，缺乏在多跳过程中迭代细化推理状态的能力。

Method: SABET-QA框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 双向实体-时间评分机制，在多步推理中双向评估实体和时间候选项；(2) 槽感知上下文模块，将问题语义与时序知识图谱嵌入对齐；(3) 可微分的记忆模块，实现渐进式假设细化；此外，当存在辅助时间边界时，将其作为粗粒度监督信号。

Result: 在CronQuestions、Complex-CronQuestions、MultiTQ和TimeQuestions四个数据集上，SABET-QA相比强基线方法表现出一致的性能提升，特别是在复杂多步时序查询上优势显著。

Conclusion: 通过多跳迭代推理与双向时间评分相结合的方式，SABET-QA有效解决了嵌入方法在TKGQA中多步时序推理的局限性，为复杂时序问答提供了一种可行的框架方案。

Abstract: Question Answering over Temporal Knowledge Graphs (TKGQA) requires reasoning over time-sensitive facts, yet existing embedding-based methods struggle with multi-step queries due to single-pass reasoning pipelines. We propose SABET-QA, a framework that iteratively refines reasoning states across multiple hops via a bidirectional entity-temporal scoring mechanism and a slot-aware contextualization module that aligns question semantics with temporal KG embeddings. A differentiable working memory enables progressive hypothesis refinement, while auxiliary temporal boundaries serve as coarse supervision when available. Experiments on CronQuestions, Complex-CronQuestions, MultiTQ, and TimeQuestions demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, particularly on complex multi-step temporal queries.

</details>


### [108] [OenoBench: A Wine-Domain Benchmark for Knowledge-Grounded Evaluation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20106)
*Nikita Khudov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍OenoBench，一个包含3,266道选择题的葡萄酒领域知识基准，覆盖六个主题和四个难度层级。该基准由38,104条来源可查的事实构建，通过LLM驱动的流水线生成题目并由九智能体审计（以Cohen's κ校准）。对16种前沿模型配置的评估发现：准确率跨度53%-84%（o3领先83.6%）；推理模式提升集中在DeepSeek R1（+6.8pp）；Anthropic有+9pp自我偏好而Google有-8pp反向偏好；开放权重模型与专有推理模型共享成本-准确率帕累托前沿；所有配置在闭卷可解项目上约提升33pp，揭示参数化回忆上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用知识基准在专业性、严谨性和可溯源方面存在不足，尤其在葡萄酒领域缺乏系统性的评估基准。作者旨在构建一个每个事实都可追溯到URL、避免LLM幻觉污染的高质量领域知识基准，以更可靠地评估语言模型在专业领域的知识能力。

Method: 方法核心包括三部分：(1) 数据构建：由35个经过来源验证的爬虫从政府注册机构（INAO、TTB、OIV）、同行评审期刊和Wikipedia/Wikidata提取38,104条原子事实；(2) 题目生成：LLM驱动的流水线中，语言模型仅负责重述已验证事实和审计结果而不作为真相来源，题目由五种生成策略在五类生成器中产生；(3) 质量审计：每个题目经九智能体审计，并通过Cohen's κ与人工金标准校准。基准覆盖六个支柱和四个难度层级。

Result: 对16种前沿模型配置的评估结果显示：整体准确率范围53%-84%，o3以83.6%领先；推理模式带来的性能提升集中在DeepSeek R1（+6.8pp），在Claude Opus和Gemini Pro中不存在；Anthropic模型在其自生成题目上出现+9pp自我偏好，而Google出现-8pp反向偏好；前沿开放权重模型与专有推理模型处于相同的成本-准确率帕累托前沿；所有配置在闭卷可解项目上约提升33pp，揭示了一个只有上下文切片才能绕过的参数化回忆上限。

Conclusion: OenoBench验证了来源锚定、LLM辅助但非LLM生成事实的基准构建范式可行且有效，并揭示出重要的模型行为特征：推理模式收益具有模型差异性、模型对自生成题目存在偏好偏差、开放权重模型的性价比竞争力以及闭卷回忆的固有上限。作者以CC-BY-SA-4.0协议开源语料、审计结果、人工审查应用和构建代码。

Abstract: We introduce OenoBench, a wine-domain knowledge benchmark of 3,266 multiple-choice questions across six pillars (regions, grape varieties, viticulture, winemaking, producers, business) and four difficulty tiers. The corpus is built from 38,104 atomic, source-anchored facts extracted by 35 provenance-verified scrapers from government registries (INAO, TTB, OIV), peer-reviewed journals, and Wikipedia/Wikidata. Our methodological contribution is an LLM-driven pipeline in which language models reformat verified facts and audit the result, but never serve as the source of truth: every claim traces to a URL, every question is generated by one of five strategies across five generator families, and every question is scored by a nine-agent audit calibrated against a human gold sheet via Cohen's $κ$. Evaluating sixteen frontier configurations, we find: (i) overall accuracy spans 53%-84%, led by o3 at 83.6%; (ii) reasoning-mode lift concentrates in DeepSeek R1 (+6.8pp) and is absent in Claude Opus and Gemini Pro; (iii) Anthropic shows +9pp self preference on its own questions while Google shows -8pp inverse preference; (iv) frontier open-weight models share the cost-vs-accuracy Pareto frontier with proprietary reasoning models; and (v) every config gains around 33pp on closed-book solvable items, revealing a parametric-recall ceiling that only the contextual slice avoids. We release corpus, audit findings, human-review app, and construction code under CC-BY-SA-4.0.

</details>


### [109] [When Text and Numbers Disagree: Evidence Arbitration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20116)
*Mattia Carletti,Edward Phillips,Fredrik K. Gustafsson,Patitapaban Palo,Lei Clifton,Danielle Belgrave,Xiao Gu,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在文本摘要、数值观测和外部工具输出等证据冲突时如何进行仲裁，通过构建受控合成基准，发现模型表现出系统性的启发式仲裁策略（如文本vs数字偏好、时间近因偏好），而非随机行为，揭示了工具增强决策系统中的一个失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地应用于需要整合多源异构证据的场景，但这些来源（文本摘要、数值数据、外部工具输出）经常提供相互矛盾的证据。当前的空白在于缺乏对被控条件下LLM如何仲裁冲突证据的系统性理解，特别是当这些来源支持相反决策时的行为模式。

Method: 作者引入了一个受控合成基准：用潜在风险轨迹同时生成数值时间序列和自然语言摘要，构建恰好只有一个证据源与真实标签一致的冲突场景。该设计允许独立操控四个因素：模态（文本vs数值）、时间近因性、来源可靠性、以及证据来源（直接上下文vs外部工具）。在多个开放权重指令微调模型上进行评测。

Result: 研究发现：1）仲裁行为是系统性的而非随机的；2）不同模型表现出明显的文本vs数字偏好差异；3）模型对时间近因性的遵循程度高于对显式可靠性线索的遵循；4）即使外部预测与直接上下文证据冲突，模型也可能过度依赖外部预测。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在整合异构证据时倾向于采用启发式仲裁策略（如时间近因、模态偏好），而非真正基于证据质量进行理性权衡。这暴露了工具增强决策系统的一个关键失败模式，提示在设计此类系统时需要更谨慎地处理证据冲突。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in settings where textual summaries, numerical observations, and external tool outputs may provide conflicting evidence. We study how LLMs arbitrate between such sources when they support opposing decisions. To do so, we introduce a controlled synthetic benchmark in which latent risk trajectories generate both numerical time series and natural language summaries, allowing us to construct conflicts where exactly one evidence source is aligned with the ground-truth label. This design lets us independently manipulate modality, temporal recency, source reliability, and evidence provenance. Across open-weight instruction-tuned models, we find that arbitration behaviour is systematic rather than random: models exhibit distinct text-versus-number preferences, follow temporal recency more consistently than explicit reliability cues, and can over-rely on external forecasts even when they conflict with direct contextual evidence. These results suggest that current LLMs often rely on heuristic arbitration strategies when integrating heterogeneous evidence, highlighting a failure mode for tool-augmented decision systems.

</details>


### [110] [FormalTCS: Benchmarking End-to-End Frontier Formal Theoretical Computer Science Research of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20153)
*Dingzirui Wang,Xuanliang Zhang,Keyan Xu,Qingfu Zhu,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个专家验证的基准测试\ourbenchmark，用于评估大语言模型在前沿端到端理论计算机科学（TCS）研究中的能力。基准包含175个来自2025-2026年STOC、FOCS、SODA和COLT论文的实例，并带有专家验证的Lean形式化证明。评估显示当前模型离完整研究流水线还有很大距离，其中自动形式化是最尖锐的瓶颈；此外，在自动研究框架中，64个生成声明仅有6个通过专家评估和证明验证，表明研究品味（research taste）是另一大障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在自动化理论计算机科学研究方面展现出潜力，但现有基准距离真实研究场景相差甚远，缺乏对前沿、端到端TCS研究能力的系统评估。

Method: 构建\ourbenchmark：包含175个来自STOC、FOCS、SODA和COLT 2025-2026年论文的实例，保留论文特定的定义、假设和证明依赖关系，并配有专家验证的Lean形式化定义和证明。基于此基准评估主流LLM的完整研究流水线能力，并开发自动化TCS研究框架以生成、形式化、筛选和证明新声明。

Result: 最佳模型在将自然语言声明翻译为形式化定理陈述上仅得分11.5，而在证明人工给定的形式化陈述时Pass@8可达28.6，说明自动形式化是最突出的瓶颈。在自动化研究框架中，64个生成声明中仅6个通过专家评估和证明验证。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在自动完成完整TCS研究流水线上仍不可靠；自动形式化能力严重不足是首要瓶颈，此外有限的研究品味（research taste）也是实现自主TCS研究的另一大障碍。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown growing potential for automated theoretical computer science (TCS) research, yet existing benchmarks remain far from realistic research settings. We introduce \ourbenchmark, an expert-validated benchmark for evaluating LLMs on frontier, end-to-end TCS research. \ourbenchmark contains $175$ instances drawn from papers accepted to STOC, FOCS, SODA, and COLT in 2025-2026, preserving paper-specific definitions, assumptions, and proof dependencies, with expert-verified Lean formalizations and proofs. Evaluations of leading LLMs reveal that current models remain far from reliably completing the full research pipeline. In particular, autoformalization is the sharpest bottleneck: the best model achieves only $11.5$ on translating natural-language claims into formal theorem statements, compared with $28.6$ Pass@8 when proving human-provided formal statements. Building on \ourbenchmark, we further develop an automated TCS research framework that generates, formalizes, filters, and proves new claims. Of $64$ generated claims, only $6$ ultimately pass expert evaluation and proof verification, indicating that beyond formalization, limited research taste remains another major barrier to autonomous TCS research.

</details>


### [111] [Task-CoEvolve: Efficient Harness Optimization via Adaptive Validation Task Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20169)
*Atsuyuki Miyai,Kiyoharu Aizawa,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Task-CoEvolve，一种通过自适应验证任务选择来优化LLM智能体harness的高效方法，在保持与全量评估相当的最终性能的同时，将优化过程中的评估次数减少80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的harness优化方法在每次迭代时都固定使用完整的验证集进行评估，但许多任务随着harness的演化会逐渐失去区分度，导致大量不必要的计算开销。因此需要一种能够动态选择有信息量的验证任务、并从部分评估中估算全量性能的方法来降低优化成本。

Method: Task-CoEvolve基于候选项在任务上出现分歧时更具信息量的观察，使用基于历史结果的方差加权采样，将评估集中在智能体能力边界附近的任务上，并随harness演化自适应调整采样分布。同时通过采样概率修正来估计全量分数，确保不同迭代间部分评估结果的可比性。

Result: 在在线文本分类和Terminal-Bench 2.1上的实验表明，Task-CoEvolve一致优于固定子集基线，与全量集搜索的最终性能相匹配，同时将优化期间的评估次数减少80%。

Conclusion: Task-CoEvolve通过任务与harness的协同演化，在保持最终性能的同时大幅降低了harness优化的评估成本，为高效的LLM智能体系统优化提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to efficient LLM agent harness optimization through adaptive validation task selection. Harness optimization iteratively rewrites the harness code based on validation performance, enabling substantial performance gains without updating the underlying model weights. Existing approaches, however, evaluate a fixed validation set in full at every iteration, incurring substantial evaluation costs even on tasks that become less discriminative as the harness evolves. We propose $\textbf{Task-CoEvolve}$, which co-evolves the validation tasks with the harness by addressing two challenges: selecting informative tasks and estimating full-set performance from partial evaluations. Task-CoEvolve builds on the observation that tasks on which candidate harnesses disagree are more informative for distinguishing among them than tasks that are consistently solved or failed. It uses variance-weighted sampling based on past outcomes to focus evaluation on tasks near the agent's capability frontier, with the sampling distribution adapting as the harness evolves. It then estimates full-set scores from the sampled tasks by accounting for their sampling probabilities, enabling consistent comparisons across iterations despite evaluating different subsets. Experiments on online text classification and Terminal-Bench 2.1 show that Task-CoEvolve consistently outperforms fixed-subset baselines and matches the final performance of full-set search while reducing the number of evaluations during optimization by 80%. Code will be released at https://github.com/Agent4Science-UTokyo/Task-CoEvolve.

</details>


### [112] [Inject, Align, Recover: Staged Post-Training for Retrieval-Free Document Knowledge Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20281)
*Qian Kou,Xiaofeng Shi,Xiaosong Qiu,Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IAR（Inject, Align, and Recover）三阶段后训练框架，用于将固定文档集合转化为可用的参数化知识，实现免检索问答。通过结构化文档知识注入、QA行为对齐和通用能力恢复三个阶段的分离设计，IAR在多个模型家族和数据集上显著优于Vanilla SFT，平均提升域QA准确率3.6个百分点，通用性能提升12.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理时未检索源文档的情况下，往往无法准确回答关于有界文档集合的问题。现有方法（如持续预训练）直接将文档注入参数知识，但缺乏结构化的注入目标和QA行为的对齐，且会损害通用能力。因此需要一个专门的三阶段框架来平衡领域知识内化与通用能力保持。

Method: IAR采用三个分离的阶段的：(1) Inject：将源文档转换为继续、改写和指令条件重建三种训练目标进行结构化文档知识注入；(2) Align：使用仅答案的QA监督信号调整注入后的模型，使其适应问答行为；(3) Recover：将领域适配模型与基础指令模型合并，恢复通用能力。实验覆盖Common Corpus（CC）和CCI数据集，以及Llama、Phi、Qwen、SmolLM四种模型家族。

Result: 在8个数据集-模型组合中，IAR在7个组合上、全部四个评估指标中均优于Vanilla SFT。平均来看，IAR在领域QA准确率上提升3.6个百分点，在IFEval、MMLU和MSBench三个通用基准上平均提升12.1个百分点。与扩展CC基线（LoRA和FAPM）比较显示，这些方法可赢得个别通用指标，但在同时达到领先或接近领先领域内化水平的方法中，IAR保持了最强的通用能力配置。

Conclusion: IAR通过将知识注入、行为对齐和能力恢复显式分离为三个阶段，有效解决了免检索文档知识内化中领域能力与通用能力之间的权衡问题，为大规模语料的参数化知识转化提供了一种更优的后训练范式。

Abstract: Large language models often fail to answer questions about a bounded document collection when the source documents are not retrieved at inference time. We study this setting as document knowledge internalization: converting a fixed corpus into usable parametric knowledge for retrieval-free question answering. We propose IAR (Inject, Align, and Recover), a three-stage post-training framework that separates structured document knowledge injection, QA behavior alignment, and general ability recovery. Unlike conventional continued pretraining, Inject converts source documents into continuation, rewrite, and instruction-conditioned reconstruction objectives. Align then adapts the injected model with answer-only QA supervision, while Recover merges the domain-adapted model with the base instruction model to recover general capabilities. Across Common Corpus (CC) and CCI, and across Llama, Phi, Qwen, and SmolLM model families, IAR improves the domain-primary domain-general frontier for retrieval-free document internalization. In the main comparison, IAR improves over Vanilla SFT on all four reported metrics in 7 of 8 dataset-model settings, with average gains of 3.6 percentage points in domain QA accuracy and 12.1 percentage points in mean general performance across IFEval, MMLU, and MSBench. Extended CC baselines show that LoRA and FAPM can win individual general metrics, but among methods that also reach leading or near-leading domain internalization, IAR retains one of the strongest general profiles.

</details>


### [113] [Inducing Task Models from Computer-Use Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20319)
*Yucheng Jiang,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Ruishi Chen,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出任务模型归纳（TMI）方法，用于从自然计算机使用轨迹中发现潜在任务并归纳结构化任务模型。TMI能解开交错并发活动，为每个潜在任务生成层次目标模型与流程控制模型。在受控人类与智能体轨迹上，TMI的任务分组一致率达0.974，重构74.9%的观测执行步骤，派生技能将留出任务准确率提升30.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然计算机使用轨迹（被动录屏与鼠标键盘操作）是推导符号化、可审计、可复用工作模型的宝贵资源，对计算机使用智能体进入实际工作场景至关重要。然而，从低层事件中归纳任务模型极具挑战：现实工作多线程且目标交错，而现有方法假设给定任务或单一工作流，且仅产生步骤级摘要而非结构化任务模型。

Method: TMI方法包含两个核心步骤：(i) 在无约束轨迹中自动发现潜在任务，解开并发交错的活动；(ii) 对每个潜在任务归纳任务模型，该模型将递归目标分解的层次目标模型与组织执行的控制流程序模型进行配对。

Result: 在受控人类与智能体轨迹上，TMI对交错任务的识别与真实分组一致率达0.974；重构了74.9%的观测执行步骤，显著超过最强工作流归纳基线。在外在评估中，由TMI任务模型派生的技能将留出任务准确率较最强基线提升30.0%。
